| `ox_ew_el_gl` | 45 | 35 | 55 | g/L | assumption |  | oxide line (sim/oxide.py) | our rounding | copper in the rich electrolyte at the set point |
| `ox_ew_emax_frac` | 1.3 | 1.1 | 1.6 | - | assumption |  | oxide line (sim/oxide.py) | our rounding | copper in the electrolyte at which the strip stops, as a multiple of the set point |
| `ox_ew_tau_h` | 1 | 0.25 | 4 | h | assumption |  | oxide line (sim/oxide.py) | our rounding | time constant of the current control (electrolyte inventory back to the set point) |
| `ox_store_cap_t` | 300 | 100 | 1000 | t | assumption |  | oxide line (sim/oxide.py) | our rounding | cathode store |
| `ox_store_high_frac` | 0.8 | 0.5 | 0.95 | - | assumption |  | oxide line (sim/oxide.py) | our rounding | store is HIGH above this share |
| `ox_ship_batch_t` | 60 | 20 | 120 | t | assumption |  | oxide line (sim/oxide.py) | our rounding | cathode in a convoy or a wagon load; shipped when the store holds a load (about a day of output) |
| `ox_store_t0` | 90 | 0 | 300 | t | assumption |  | oxide line (sim/oxide.py) | our rounding | cathode in the store at the start of a run |
| `ox_low_frac` | 0.1 | 0.05 | 0.2 | - | assumption |  | oxide line (sim/oxide.py) | our rounding | sxew-1 is LOW when the cathode is below the expected one by more than this share |
| `ox_rect_fix_h` | 2 | 0.5 | 8 | h | assumption |  | oxide line (sim/oxide.py) | our rounding | restart of a tripped rectifier by the electrician after the arrival |
| `ox_rect_auto_h` | 8 | 2 | 24 | h | assumption |  | oxide line (sim/oxide.py) | our rounding | the rectifier is found and restarted by itself this long after the trip if nobody is called |
| `ox_crud_ok_cm` | 0.5 | 0.2 | 1 | cm | assumption |  | oxide line (sim/oxide.py) | our rounding | crud layer in the settler with the plant in order |
| `ox_crud_alert_cm` | 3 | 1.5 | 6 | cm | assumption |  | oxide line (sim/oxide.py) | our rounding | crud layer at which the operator is told |
| `ox_crud_crit_cm` | 8 | 4 | 15 | cm | assumption |  | oxide line (sim/oxide.py) | our rounding | crud layer at which the extraction is at its worst |
| `ox_crud_rise_h` | 5 | 1 | 12 | h | assumption |  | oxide line (sim/oxide.py) | our rounding | hours from the normal layer to the alert |
| `ox_crud_fail_h` | 6 | 1 | 12 | h | assumption |  | oxide line (sim/oxide.py) | our rounding | hours from the alert to the worst layer |
| `ox_crud_fix_h` | 3 | 1 | 8 | h | assumption |  | oxide line (sim/oxide.py) | our rounding | removal of crud (treatment of the organic phase) after the arrival |
| `ox_crud_eta_frac` | 0.6 | 0.3 | 0.9 | - | assumption |  | oxide line (sim/oxide.py) | our rounding | extraction as a share of the normal one at the worst layer |
| `ox_crud_q_frac` | 0.5 | 0.2 | 0.9 | - | assumption |  | oxide line (sim/oxide.py) | our rounding | SX feed flow that the settler can take, as a share of the normal one, at the worst layer |
| `lev_feed_min_pct` | 80 | 80 | 80 | % | assumption |  | levers (plant, service) | stand lever (what-if control of the model, not of the plant); bounds are a design choice of the stand | lever: lowest feed rate of a SAG line, % of the nominal line rate |
| `lev_feed_max_pct` | 120 | 120 | 120 | % | assumption |  | levers (plant, service) | stand lever (what-if control of the model, not of the plant); bounds are a design choice of the stand | lever: highest feed rate of a SAG line, % of the nominal line rate (the power limit, liner wear and the pebble circuit still bind) |
| `lev_p80_min_um` | 160 | 160 | 160 | um | assumption |  | levers (plant, service) | stand lever (what-if control of the model, not of the plant); bounds are a design choice of the stand; Satbayev University repository (Bozshakol plant, daily data 2022-04.2025, regression), table 2 p. 61 and tables 4-6 p. 62: -1.95 pp Cu recovery per +10 um P80 (plant model); lab -1.2 pp per 10 um (about -7.3 pp over 60 um): https://repository.satbayev.university/api/files/ep83fzrdl7c6ztx7jeo3dj51w.pdf | lever: lowest grind P80 target |
| `lev_p80_max_um` | 200 | 200 | 200 | um | assumption |  | levers (plant, service) | stand lever (what-if control of the model, not of the plant); bounds are a design choice of the stand; Satbayev University repository (Bozshakol plant, daily data 2022-04.2025, regression), table 2 p. 61 and tables 4-6 p. 62: -1.95 pp Cu recovery per +10 um P80 (plant model); lab -1.2 pp per 10 um (about -7.3 pp over 60 um): https://repository.satbayev.university/api/files/ep83fzrdl7c6ztx7jeo3dj51w.pdf | lever: highest grind P80 target |
| `lev_p80_pp_10um` | 1.2 | 1.2 | 1.95 | pp | analog |  | levers (plant, service) | Satbayev University repository (Bozshakol plant, daily data 2022-04.2025, regression), table 2 p. 61 and tables 4-6 p. 62: -1.95 pp Cu recovery per +10 um P80 (plant model); lab -1.2 pp per 10 um (about -7.3 pp over 60 um): https://repository.satbayev.university/api/files/ep83fzrdl7c6ztx7jeo3dj51w.pdf | lever: total Cu recovery per 10 um of P80 around flot_p80_ref_um (finer gains, coarser loses): the lab slope of the Bozshakol study; the plant regression of the same study is lev_p80_pp_plant_10um |
| `lev_p80_pp_plant_10um` | 1.95 | 1.95 | 1.95 | pp | analog |  | levers (plant, service) | Satbayev University repository (Bozshakol plant, daily data 2022-04.2025, regression), table 2 p. 61 and tables 4-6 p. 62: -1.95 pp Cu recovery per +10 um P80 (plant model); lab -1.2 pp per 10 um (about -7.3 pp over 60 um): https://repository.satbayev.university/api/files/ep83fzrdl7c6ztx7jeo3dj51w.pdf | upper end of the shown range: plant regression of Bozshakol, -1.95 pp per +10 um (shown, not used by the model) |
| `lev_dose_min_gt` | 15 | 15 | 15 | g/t | assumption |  | levers (plant, service) | stand lever (what-if control of the model, not of the plant); bounds are a design choice of the stand | lever: lowest collector dose |
| `lev_dose_max_gt` | 40 | 40 | 40 | g/t | assumption |  | levers (plant, service) | stand lever (what-if control of the model, not of the plant); bounds are a design choice of the stand | lever: highest collector dose |
| `lev_dose_conc_pp` | 1 | 0 | 3 | pp | assumption |  | levers (plant, service) | Putra et al., Rekayasa Material, Manufaktur dan Energi 7(1) 2024, pp. 27-34 (plant scale, PT. X Papua, SIBX 5/10/15 g/t: Cu recovery does not rise with the dose; gangue recovery 7.5 -> 23 -> 88 %): https://jurnal.umsu.ac.id/index.php/RMME/article/download/16941/10615 | lever: concentrate grade lost to dilution at +40 % over the optimum dose (qualitative in the source: gangue recovery rises with the dose; the size is an assumption) |
| `lev_reagent_usd_t` | 2000 | 1500 | 3500 | USD/t | assumption |  | levers (plant, service) | price of the collector (same basis as reagent_usd_kg); the Volza page of PAX export prices 1 770-2 000 USD/t (2023) did not open (403) and is not used as a source | lever: collector price for the extra (or saved) reagent of the dose lever |

| Line | Date | Kind | Label | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 2024-03-15 | reline | fact | KAZ Minerals Q1 2024 production report, p. 3: https://www.kazminerals.com/media/23119/q1-2024-production-report-final.pdf: planned maintenance of the quarter, 'including a SAG mill reline'; the quarter is the report's, the month and the day are assumed (line 1 tonnes rose in Q2: 6 921 vs 6 751 kt) |
| 2 | 2024-10-15 | sag_service | fact | KAZ Minerals Q4 2024 production report (FY2024), p. 3: https://www.kazminerals.com/media/23240/q4-2024-production-report_final.pdf: planned SAG maintenance at Sulphide 2 in October 2024, line 2 tonnes -4 % (7 244 vs 7 572 kt); 'reline' is not the report's word; day assumed |
| 2 | 2025-08-15 | reline | fact | KAZ Minerals Q3 2025 production report, p. 3: https://www.kazminerals.com/app/uploads/2025/12/q3-2025-production-report_final.pdf: SAG reline at Sulphide 2 in August 2025, named in the report; day assumed |
| 1 | 2025-12-05 | stop | fact | KAZ Minerals Q4 2025 production report (FY2025), p. 3: https://www.kazminerals.com/app/uploads/2026/02/Q4-2025-Production-report_FINAL_ENG.pdf: planned maintenance at both concentrators in December 2025, kind of work not disclosed; SAG reline and day are assumptions |
| 2 | 2025-12-15 | stop | fact | KAZ Minerals Q4 2025 production report (FY2025), p. 3: https://www.kazminerals.com/app/uploads/2026/02/Q4-2025-Production-report_FINAL_ENG.pdf: planned maintenance at both concentrators in December 2025, kind of work not disclosed; SAG reline and day are assumptions |

## Feature rules

| Type | Feature | Rule |
|---|---|---|
| site | `state` | RUN/PAUSED/WARMUP (service: run control) |
| site | `speed` | service: requested model speed |
| site | `speed_fact` | service: achieved speed |
| site | `seed` | run seed |
| site | `tph` | sum of line t/h |
| site | `cu_tph` | sum of line Cu in concentrate t/h |
| site | `power_mw` | power.site_mw |
| site | `water_m3h` | water.intake_m3h |
| site | `scenario` | active scenario text |
| site | `heartbeat` | service: wall clock tick in pause |
| site | `degraded` | service: barrier timeout flag |
| site | `lever_on` | 1 while a what-if lever (control of the model, not of the plant) is in force on the fact copy |
| site | `lever_feed_1` | lever: feed rate of SAG line 1, % of the nominal line rate (100 = neutral) |
| site | `lever_feed_2` | lever: feed rate of SAG line 2, % of the nominal line rate (100 = neutral) |
| site | `lever_p80_um` | lever: grind P80 target, um (-1 = off) |
| site | `lever_dose_gt` | lever: collector dose, g/t (-1 = off) |
| site | `state_ru` | state in Russian words (display) |
| pit | `state` | RUN/STOP |
| pit | `mining_tph` | fleet.mining_tph |
| pit | `next_block` | next block number in plan |
| pit | `plan_text` | 12 h plan of blocks per line |
| pit | `hard_in_plan` | 1 if est A*b < ui_axb_hard in plan |
| pit | `visibility_m` | visibility on the haul roads (pit_vis_clear_m; a snowstorm spell lowers it to pit_vis_icy_m) |
| pit | `road_grip` | friction index of the haul roads 0..1 (dry, winter season with ice, snowstorm spell) |
| pit | `haul_speed_pct` | haul speed against the dry road, % (100 / truck cycle multiplier of weather and restriction) |
| pit | `road_restrict` | 1 while the traffic restriction (speed limit) is in force after the decision of the mine dispatcher |
| pit | `state_ru` | state in Russian words (display) |
| exc | `state` | DIG/MOVE/IDLE/DOWN |
| exc | `tph` | dig rate |
| exc | `block` | batch being dug |
| exc | `bench` | bench number |
| exc | `oil_pct` | hydraulic oil contamination, % of the alert level of the on-machine sensor (EX3600-6 has one); -1 where no source confirms a sensor |
| exc | `oil_alert` | 1 from the alert level of the sensor (the operator sees it; transmitted only with the optional Global e-Service), -1 without a sensor |
| exc | `track_press_pct` | accumulator pressure of the track tension, % of the preset auto-stop level (EX3600-6 only, -1 elsewhere) |
| exc | `state_ru` | state in Russian words (display) |
| batch | `state` | PLANNED/DIG/BELT/IN_STOCK/DONE by the last tonne: DIG while any is in the pit, BELT while any is in transit, IN_STOCK when all is on the stockpile |
| batch | `location` | current parent thing |
| batch | `tonnes_left` | t not yet fed to SAG (weigher accounting) |
| batch | `pit_t` | t still in the pit (not dug) |
| batch | `cu_meas` | lab feed Cu of the sample taken while the batch led the SAG feed |
| batch | `axb_soft` | soft sensor A*b from SAG P and Q |
| batch | `state_ru` | state in Russian words (display) |
| crusher | `state` | RUN/STOP/SLOW |
| crusher | `tph` | min(pocket, capacity, stock room) |
| crusher | `power_kw` | crush_power_mw when RUN |
| crusher | `lube_kpa` | norm + noise; trip below setpoint |
| crusher | `service` | 1 while the planned change of the mantle stops the crusher (scenario mantle:N) |
| crusher | `service_left_h` | hours to the end of the mantle change (plan), -1 when none |
| crusher | `state_ru` | state in Russian words (display) |
| conveyor | `state` | RUN/STOP |
| conveyor | `tph` | = crusher t/h |
| conveyor | `power_kw` | conv_power_mw when RUN |
| conveyor | `fault` | 1 while the belt is tripped (scenario conveyor:N, crew-cpd repairs); 0 when it stands only because no ore comes |
| conveyor | `idler_temp_c` | bearing temperature of the hottest idler, degC (thermal scan; healthy w_idler_temp_ok_c) |
| conveyor | `state_ru` | state in Russian words (display) |
| stockpile | `state` | NORMAL/LOW/EMPTY/FULL: LOW below stock_low_frac, EMPTY below stock_empty_t, FULL at stock_full_frac of stock_live_t |
| stockpile | `level_t` | column + sides |
| stockpile | `in_tph` | belt delivery |
| stockpile | `out_tph` | SAG draw |
| stockpile | `hours_left` | level / (out - in) when falling, else -1 |
| stockpile | `state_ru` | state in Russian words (display) |
| line | `state` | RUN/LIMITED/STOP: STOP while the SAG is RELINE or STOP, LIMITED under a power cap or with one ball mill |
| line | `tph` | SAG fresh feed |
| line | `cu_tph` | Cu in concentrate |
| line | `recovery` | flot recovery |
| line | `cum_cu_t` | shift Cu |
| line | `cum_mwh` | shift energy |
| line | `cum_m3` | shift water intake share |
| line | `cum_usd` | shift value: payable Cu - energy - water |
| line | `cum_cu_t_total` | run total of cum_cu_t (never reset) |
| line | `cum_mwh_total` | run total of cum_mwh |
| line | `cum_m3_total` | run total of cum_m3 |
| line | `cum_usd_total` | run total of cum_usd |
| line | `balls_gt` | grinding media consumption of the line (scale reading), g/t of ore |
| line | `state_ru` | state in Russian words (display) |
| sag | `state` | RUN/STARVED/LIMITED_GRID/RELINE/STOP |
| sag | `power_kw` | Austin form of load and speed (shaft), not below the ball-charge draw |
| sag | `power_limit_kw` | min(rated, grid cap) |
| sag | `feed_tph` | Q = min(stock, eta P / E, pebble limit) |
| sag | `speed_pct` | setpoint; lowered when a power cap is below the ball-charge draw |
| sag | `load_pct` | mass balance, tau 5 min; not below the ball charge; kept in STOP |
| sag | `e_kwh_t` | power / Q |
| sag | `axb_soft` | A*b back-solved from E |
| sag | `liner_wear_mm` | feed-end counter by norm 20.41 mm/Mt; restarts at feed-end changes and relines |
| sag | `liner_ground_t` | ore ground since the last reline, t (tonnage counter by norm, restarts at a reline); the module forecasts the liner campaign from it |
| sag | `gmd_gap_mm` | air gap of the gearless drive, mm (healthy w_gmd_gap_ok_mm) |
| sag | `state_ru` | state in Russian words (display) |
| ball | `state` | RUN/STOP |
| ball | `power_kw` | ball_power_mw when RUN |
| ball | `start_cmd` | 1 while a start command is on although people may be inside (scenario startup) |
| ball | `e_kwh_t` | power x running mills / Q (each running mill grinds Q / n) |
| ball | `power_pct` | motor draw as % of the rating: 100 in normal grinding, up by ball_rope_over_pct at full cyclone roping (overload), 0 while the mill stands |
| ball | `state_ru` | state in Russian words (display) |
| cyclones | `state` | NORMAL/UPSET: UPSET if |dp/p0| above cyc_upset_frac |
| cyclones | `p80_um` | Bond grind (not finer than ball_p80_min_um) + 12 um per clogged unit |
| cyclones | `pressure_kpa` | p0 (Q/open)^2 x clog rise |
| cyclones | `open_count` | open units (clog not visible) |
| cyclones | `circ_load_pct` | circulating load of the ball mill circuit (underflow / fresh feed), cyc_circ_base_pct, up by cyc_rope_circ_frac at full roping |
| cyclones | `pump_head_pct` | discharge pressure of the cyclone feed pump, % of normal (cavitation, a worn impeller lower it) |
| cyclones | `state_ru` | state in Russian words (display) |
| flot | `state` | RUN/UPSET/STOP |
| flot | `feed_cu` | analyser |
| flot | `tail_cu` | analyser |
| flot | `conc_cu` | analyser |
| flot | `recovery` | R_r x R_c |
| flot | `cu_tph` | Cu in concentrate |
| flot | `conc_tph` | concentrate solids |
| flot | `level_swing_pct` | swing of the cell level, % of the controlled span (healthy w_froth_swing_ok_pct) |
| flot | `state_ru` | state in Russian words (display) |
| rougher | `state` | RUN/UPSET/STOP |
| rougher | `feed_tph` | line Q |
| rougher | `stage_recovery` | R_inf (1 - exp(-k tau)) |
| rougher | `mass_pull_pct` | 7 % x g / g0 |
| rougher | `rconc_cu` | rougher concentrate grade |
| rougher | `residence_min` | tau0 x Q0 / Q |
| rougher | `dose_sp_gt` | setpoint |
| rougher | `dose_meas_gt` | flowmeter reading |
| rougher | `tank_t` | collector day tank, load cells (noise sens_tank_sigma_t) |
| rougher | `tank_in_t_total` | deliveries into the tank since the start (booked, exact) |
| rougher | `dose_t_total` | flowmeter totalizer: reading x Q x dt since the start |
| rougher | `state_ru` | state in Russian words (display) |
| cleaner | `state` | RUN/UPSET/STOP |
| cleaner | `feed_tph` | rougher concentrate |
| cleaner | `stage_recovery` | 97 % - 1 pp per 10 pp extra pull |
| cleaner | `conc_cu` | 24.3 - 0.5 (R - R0 - feed-grade gain) |
| cleaner | `conc_tph` | g R / grade |
| cleaner | `tail_cu` | cleaner tails grade |
| cleaner | `state_ru` | state in Russian words (display) |
| water | `state` | NORMAL/LOW/SHORT/ALARM: ALARM at piezo_kpa >= pond_piezo_sp_kpa, SHORT while the fresh water is at its supply cap and the ore is cut, LOW below pond_lo_m3 |
| water | `intake_m3h` | process - recycle (not above water_intake_cap_m3h) |
| water | `recycle_m3h` | pond return delivered + thickener overflow |
| water | `tails_loss_m3h` | 0.31 m3/t pore water of settled tails |
| water | `evap_m3h` | fitted |
| water | `pond_m3` | integrated |
| water | `residual_pct` | balance closure |
| water | `piezo_kpa` | a x pond + noise |
| water | `intake_m3_total` | intake flowmeter totalizer since the start (noisy rate x dt) |
| water | `loss_m3_total` | tails pores + concentrate moisture + evaporation, integrated (a leak is not metered: it shows as the balance residual) |
| water | `leak_m3h` | return water lost through a pipeline leak: truth only, measured -1 (no meter on a leak) |
| water | `air_temp_c` | weather: season mean + daily swing |
| water | `wind_ms` | weather: hourly draw |
| water | `beach_dry_pct` | dry share of the tailings beach (season, wind spell) |
| water | `winter` | 1 while the winter return loss is on (season or scenario winter) |
| water | `short_m3h` | process water missing above the intake cap |
| water | `cut_tph` | ore held back on both lines for lack of water |
| water | `intake_cap_m3h` | fresh-water supply cap |
| water | `state_ru` | state in Russian words (display) |
| power | `state` | NORMAL/LIMITED: LIMITED while the grid limit is below the normal limit |
| power | `grid_limit_mw` | 300 or scenario |
| power | `site_mw` | mills (grid) + crushers + conveyors + other |
| power | `grinding_mw` | mills from grid |
| power | `grinding_shaft_mw` | mills on shaft |
| power | `other_mw` | fitted site part + pow_line_aux_mw per running line |
| power | `shed_mw` | load shedding |
| power | `headroom_mw` | limit - site |
| power | `shed_text` | connections shed by the automatic load shedding, in order (codes) |
| power | `state_ru` | state in Russian words (display) |
| crew | `state` | FREE/TRAVEL/WORK/OFF |
| crew | `task` | issue node |
| crew | `free_at` | model minute when free |
| crew | `state_ru` | state in Russian words (display) |
| fleet | `state` | NORMAL/SHORT: SHORT when haul_tph is below the mining need |
| fleet | `exc_total` | count (ore and stripping) |
| fleet | `exc_ready` | not DOWN |
| fleet | `trucks_total` | 37 by model |
| fleet | `trucks_ready` | each truck ready with fleet_kio, drawn hourly |
| fleet | `truck_kio_shift` | shift mean ready share |
| fleet | `haul_tph` | ready ore trucks x payload x 60 / cycle |
| fleet | `mining_tph` | min(excavators, haul, regulator) |
| fleet | `trucks_ore` | ready trucks on ore |
| fleet | `waste_tph` | stripping rate (waste trucks and excavators) |
| fleet | `fatigue` | 1 while the alert of a tired driver / close approach of a truck is on (scenario fatigue; assumption: no confirmed source for the sensor) |
| fleet | `fatigue_truck` | bort number and model of the truck in the alert, empty when none |
| fleet | `brake_oil_c` | brake oil temperature of the truck HT141 (CAT 793D, VIMS), degC |
| fleet | `brake_truck` | bort number of the truck whose brake oil is shown |
| fleet | `state_ru` | state in Russian words (display) |
| pebble | `state` | RUN/LIMIT/STOP |
| pebble | `feed_tph` | p(A*b) x Q |
| pebble | `limit_tph` | 740 (line 2: min with its HPGR 1 061) |
| pebble | `load_pct` | feed / limit |
| pebble | `fire` | 1 while a fire at the HPGR of the circuit is not out |
| pebble | `hpgr_tph` | rated HPGR throughput (line 2: peb_hpgr_l2_tph; line 1: no published rating, -1) |
| pebble | `hpgr_load_pct` | feed / hpgr_tph (line 2), -1 on line 1 |
| pebble | `hpgr_kw` | peb_hpgr_l2_kw x min(1, feed / hpgr_tph), 0 while the circuit stands, -1 on line 1 |
| pebble | `hpgr_skew_pct` | roll gap skew of the HPGR, % of the mean gap (line 2), 0 in normal feed, -1 on line 1 |
| pebble | `hpgr_kw_diff_pct` | difference of the power of the two HPGR drives, % (line 2), -1 on line 1 |
| pebble | `metal_alarm` | 1 when the metal detector in front of the HPGR tripped (line 2), -1 on line 1 |
| pebble | `state_ru` | state in Russian words (display) |
| person | `state` | FREE/TRAVEL/WORK/OFF: OFF while evacuated or off shift |
| person | `location` | thing where the person is (home node when free) |
| person | `task` | node of the job or decision |
| person | `free_at` | model minute when free |
| person | `permit` | node of the work permit (nariad-dopusk) while the person works inside equipment, else empty |
| person | `state_ru` | state in Russian words (display) |
| truck | `state` | LOADING/HAUL_FULL/QUEUE/DUMP/RETURN/DOWN: cycle phase, QUEUE while its line digs nothing |
| truck | `location` | face, road or crusher |
| truck | `load_t` | payload on board |
| truck | `cycle_min` | trip time now |
| truck | `duty` | ore line N or waste |
| truck | `state_ru` | state in Russian words (display) |
| face | `state` | ACTIVE/IDLE/DOWN by its excavator |
| face | `block` | batch being dug |
| face | `est_cu` | block-model Cu of the block |
| face | `est_axb` | block-model A*b |
| face | `hardness` | class by A*b |
| face | `bench` | bench number |
| face | `tph` | dig rate |
| face | `blasted_t` | blasted rock ready to dig: + drill rigs (holes x t per hole), - excavator |
| face | `state_ru` | state in Russian words (display) |
| drill | `state` | DRILLING/MOVING/IDLE/DOWN: IDLE while its face holds pit_face_blasted_max_t |
| drill | `location` | face drilled |
| drill | `holes_shift` | holes drilled this shift |
